user.role | role_label }}).</p>\n     5\t  {% if user.role == \"admin\" %}\n     6\t    <p><a href=\"/admin/users\">Manage users</a></p>\n     7\t  {% endif %}\n     8\t  <form method=\"post\" action=\"/logout\">\n     9\t    <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token(request) }}\">\n    10\t    <button type=\"submit\">Log out</button>\n    11\t  </form>\n    12\t{% endblock %}\n=== templates/admin/users.html\n     1\t{% extends \"base.html\" %}\n     2\t{% block content %}\n     3\t  <h1>Users</h1>\n     4\t  {% if error %}<p role=\"alert\">{{ error }}</p>{% endif %}\n     5\t  <table>\n     6\t    <thead><tr><th>Name</th><th>Email</th><th>Role</th><th>Status</th></tr></thead>\n     7\t    <tbody>\n     8\t    {% for u in users %}\n     9\t      <tr id=\"user-{{ u.id }}\">\n    10\t        <td>{{ u.name }}{% if u.id == user.id %} (you){% endif %}</td>\n    11\t        <td>{{ u.email }}</td>\n    12\t        {# No controls on your own row (you can't demote or deactivate yourself)\n    13\t           or on a deactivated one. #}\n    14\t        {% if u.id == user.id or not u.is_active %}\n    15\t        <td>{{ u.role | role_label }}</td>\n    16\t        <td>{{ \"Active\" if u.is_active else \"Deactivated\" }}</td>\n    17\t        {% else %}\n    18\t        <td>\n    19\t          <form method=\"post\" action=\"/admin/users/{{ u.id }}/role\">\n    20\t            <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token(request) }}\">\n    21\t            <select name=\"role\" aria-label=\"Role for {{ u.email }}\">\n    22\t              {% for value, label in role_labels.items() %}\n    23\t                <option value=\"{{ value }}\"{% if value == u.role %} selected{% endif %}>{{ label }}</option>\n    24\t              {% endfor %}\n    25\t            </select>\n    26\t            <button type=\"submit\">Change role</button>\n    27\t          </form>\n    28\t        </td>\n    29\t        <td>\n    30\t          Active\n    31\t          <form method=\"post\" action=\"/admin/users/{{ u.id }}/deactivate\">\n    32\t            <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token(request) }}\">\n    33\t            <button type=\"submit\">Deactivate</button>\n    34\t          </form>\n    35\t        </td>\n    36\t        {% endif %}\n    37\t      </tr>\n    38\t    {% endfor %}\n    39\t    </tbody>\n    40\t  </table>\n    41\t\n    42\t  <h2>Add a user</h2>\n    43\t  <form method=\"post\" action=\"/admin/users\">\n    44\t    <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token(request) }}\">\n    45\t    <p><label>Name <input type=\"text\" name=\"name\" value=\"{{ form.name }}\" required></label></p>\n    46\t    <p><label>Email <input type=\"email\" name=\"email\" value=\"{{ form.email }}\" required></label></p>\n    47\t    <p><label>Password <input type=\"password\" name=\"password\" required minlength=\"{{ min_password_length }}\" autocomplete=\"new-password\"></label></p>\n    48\t    <p><label>Role\n    49\t      <select name=\"role\">\n    50\t        {% for value, label in role_labels.items() %}\n    51\t          <option value=\"{{ value }}\"{% if value == form.role | default(\"editor\") %} selected{% endif %}>{{ label }}</option>\n    52\t        {% endfor %}\n    53\t      </select>\n    54\t    </label></p>\n    55\t    <p><button type=\"submit\">Add user</button></p>\n    56\t  </form>\n    57\t{% endblock %}\n=== templates/public/home.html\n     1\t{% extends \"base.html\" %}\n     2\t{% block content %}\n     3\t  <h1>{{ title }}</h1>\n     4\t  {% if items %}\n     5\t    <ul>{% for item in items %}<li><a href=\"{{ item.href }}\">{{ item.title }}</a></li>{% endfor %}</ul>\n     6\t  {% else %}\n     7\t    <p>Nothing published yet.</p>\n     8\t  {% endif %}\n     9\t{% endblock %}\n=== docs/adr/ADR-003-split-publish-rights.md\n     1\t# Analysts publish Data Bites directly; only the Director publishes Reports; each intern gets their own Analyst account\n     2\t\n     3\t**Status:** accepted (supersedes an earlier draft of this ADR that proposed one shared Analyst account)\n     4\t\n     5\tAnalysts (interns) currently email IR staff or drop files in a shared\n     6\tOneDrive folder to get anything posted, which is the exact bottleneck this\n     7\tCMS exists to remove. But Reports (Factbook, Common Data Set, Survey Policy)\n     8\tget cited externally and must stay accurate and stable, so we don't want\n     9\tintern turnover or a drafting mistake taking one live unreviewed.\n    10\t\n    11\tWe decided: the editor permission is split by content type, not uniform.\n    12\tAnalysts can create, edit, and **publish** a Data Bite without approval.\n    13\tAnalysts can create and edit a Report, but only the Director (admin) can\n    14\tpublish or edit a Report once it exists — even one an Analyst drafted.\n    15\t\n    16\t**Accounts:** each intern gets their own named Analyst account, not a shared\n    17\tlogin. This reverses an earlier version of this decision. Trying WordPress\n    18\tPlayground surfaced the reason: a shared account makes every Data Bite's\n    19\tauthor say \"Analyst,\" with no way to tell which intern actually wrote it,\n    20\twhich felt wrong the moment it was compared against a real WordPress content\n    21\tlist showing a real name. Individual accounts cost the Director a few extra\n    22\tminutes of user management per intern rotation (create/deactivate each\n    23\tsemester) — worth it for honest attribution.\n    24\t\n    25\t**Shift continuity is preserved despite individual accounts:** any Analyst\n    26\tmay edit any other Analyst's draft (Data Bite or draft Report), so a\n    27\thalf-finished item from last week's intern doesn't need Director\n    28\tintervention to pick back up. Editing preserves the original author and\n    29\t`created_at`; only `updated_at` changes. This means \"Analyst\" is a role, not\n    30\tan ownership boundary — the boundary that matters is Analyst vs. Director,\n    31\tnot Analyst-A vs. Analyst-B.\n    32\t\n    33\tThe alternative (uniform editor rights, or a Director-approves-everything\n    34\tgate) was rejected: the former risks an intern accidentally publishing a\n    35\tFactbook revision, the latter reintroduces the \"email IR to post something\"\n    36\tbottleneck for the routine, low-stakes case.","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"1cb138ab-d9ba-4f0e-8db5-f7b47daa3e77","session_id":"fc138a42-1eee-4017-bff2-c6a77302f820","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/davelleat/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"fc138a42-1eee-4017-bff2-c6a77302f820","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"9fe180da-2117-4a39-a089-0f36f551a342","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"fe935dab-cbcb-458c-aa72-102088c73805","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01U5Gn3ESumG38EvXg5VRgkU","type":"tool_result","content":"T06: Admin console\n## Parent\n\nPart of #1\n\n## What to build\n\nDashboard with real counts of Data Bites/Reports by status, a content list filterable by type and status, and a Markdown preview (sanitized) while editing either content type.\n\n## Acceptance criteria\n\n- [ ] Dashboard shows accurate counts of Data Bites and Reports broken down by draft/published\n- [ ] Content list can be filtered by content type\n- [ ] Content list can be filtered by status\n- [ ] Editing a Data Bite or a Report shows a live preview of the sanitized rendered Markdown\n- [ ] Anonymous visitor is redirected to login for all of the above\n- [ ] Tests cover all of the above\n\n## Blocked by\n\n- #4 (T03)\n- #5 (T04)\n\nT07: Public site pages\n## Parent\n\nPart of #1\n\n## What to build\n\nThe live public-facing pages, served by the admin console's own FastAPI app (not yet the static export): a list/page for published Data Bites, a list/page for published Reports, and site navigation built from published Reports. Only published content is ever visible.\n\n## Acceptance criteria\n\n- [ ] Public visitor can view a list of published Data Bites and an individual Data Bite's page\n- [ ] Public visitor can view a list of published Reports and an individual Report's page (Markdown body, plus a download link if a file is attached)\n- [ ] Site navigation lists only published Reports\n- [ ] A draft Data Bite or Report (or its file) is never visible or linked anywhere on these public pages\n- [ ] Tests cover all of the above\n\n## Blocked by\n\n- #4 (T03)\n- #5 (T04)\n\n# Security checklist\n\nWhat must be true before you submit. *How* to make each one true is your work —\nthat is the point of the tickets.\n\n| # | Must be true | How the grader checks it |\n|---|---|---|\n| 1 | Stored passwords are argon2 hashes | Reads the seeded database and looks at the stored value |\n| 2 | Wrong passwords are refused, and the message does not reveal whether the account exists | Tries a bad password |\n| 3 | Every state-changing form (POST) carries a CSRF token and rejects a request without one | Replays each POST with the token removed |\n| 4 | An editor cannot reach any admin-only route | Logs in as the seeded editor and requests each admin route |\n| 5 | An anonymous visitor is redirected to login, never shown admin content | Requests admin routes with no session |\n| 6 | A deactivated user cannot log in | Deactivates the seeded editor, then tries to log in |\n| 7 | User-written Markdown cannot inject scripts | Saves a post containing `<script>` and an `onerror=` attribute, publishes, and checks both the preview and the export |\n| 8 | Drafts never appear in `site/` or on the public site | Publishes with a draft present and searches the output |\n| 9 | No secrets, databases, or `.env` files in git history | Greps the full history |\n| 10 | Access control is enforced in code, not by hiding links | Requests admin URLs directly rather than clicking |\n\nRule of thumb: if the only thing stopping an editor from reaching a page is that\nthe link is hidden, it is not stopping anyone.\n.env\n*.db\nsite/\n__pycache__/\n.pytest_cache/\n.venv/\n.claude/state/\n.DS_Store","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"98372b8c-e63c-44d0-991c-e9a61f96ff87","timestamp":"2026-09-28T22:02:41.292Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"T06: Only published content is ever visible.\n\n## Acceptance criteria\n\n- [ ] Public visitor can view a list of published Data Bites and an individual Data Bite's page\n- [ ] Public visitor can view a list of published Reports and an individual Report's page (Markdown body, plus a download link if a file is attached)\n- [ ] Site navigation lists only published Reports\n- [ ] A draft Data Bite or Report (or its file) is never visible or linked anywhere on these public pages\n- [ ] Tests cover all of the above\n\n## Blocked by\n\n- #4 (T03)\n- #5 (T04)\n\n# Security checklist\n\nWhat must be true before you submit. *How* to make each one true is your work —\nthat is the point of the tickets.\n\n| # | Must be true | How the grader checks it |\n|---|---|---|\n| 1 | Stored passwords are argon2 hashes | Reads the seeded database and looks at the stored value |\n| 2 | Wrong passwords are refused, and the message does not reveal whether the account exists | Tries a bad password |\n| 3 | Every state-changing form (POST) carries a CSRF token and rejects a request without one | Replays each POST with the token removed |\n| 4 | An editor cannot reach any admin-only route | Logs in as the seeded editor and requests each admin route |\n| 5 | An anonymous visitor is redirected to login, never shown admin content | Requests admin routes with no session |\n| 6 | A deactivated user cannot log in | Deactivates the seeded editor, then tries to log in |\n| 7 | User-written Markdown cannot inject scripts | Saves a post containing `<script>` and an `onerror=` attribute, publishes, and checks both the preview and the export |\n| 8 | Drafts never appear in `site/` or on the public site | Publishes with a draft present and searches the output |\n| 9 | No secrets, databases, or `.env` files in git history | Greps the full history |\n| 10 | Access control is enforced in code, not by hiding links | Requests admin URLs directly rather than clicking |\n\nRule of thumb: if the only thing stopping an editor from reaching a page is that\nthe link is hidden, it is not stopping anyone.\n.env\n*.db\nsite/\n__pycache__/\n.pytest_cache/\n.venv/\n.claude/state/\n.DS_Store","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"9fe180da-2117-4a39-a089-0f36f551a342","session_id":"fc138a42-1eee-4017-bff2-c6a77302f820","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/davelleat/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"fc138a42-1eee-4017-bff2-c6a77302f820","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"4db0adb4-c4d7-4e1e-9984-a766d309f8d1","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"fe935dab-cbcb-458c-aa72-102088c73805","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"tool_result","tool_use_id":"toolu_01SpnZdZfLxcMZx1AEqSTY5q","content":"Launching skill: mattpocock-skills:tdd"}]},"uuid":"e7e968c5-ee0a-4b5f-917e-005eac336c24","timestamp":"2026-09-28T22:03:02.955Z","toolUseResult":{"success":true,"commandName":"mattpocock-skills:tdd"},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"4db0adb4-c4d7-4e1e-9984-a766d309f8d1","session_id":"fc138a42-1eee-4017-bff2-c6a77302f820","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/davelleat/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"fc138a42-1eee-4017-bff2-c6a77302f820","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"e7e968c5-ee0a-4b5f-917e-005eac336c24","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"fe935dab-cbcb-458c-aa72-102088c73805","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Base directory for this skill: /Users/davelleat/.claude/plugins/cache/claude-plugins-official/mattpocock-skills/1.2.3/skills/engineering/tdd\n\n# Test-Driven Development\n\nTDD is the red → green loop. This skill is the reference that makes that loop produce tests worth keeping: what a good test is, where tests go, the anti-patterns, and the rules of the loop. Every section applies on every cycle: consult them before and during the loop, not after.\n\nWhen exploring the codebase, read `CONTEXT.md` (if it exists) so test names and interface vocabulary match the project's domain language, and respect ADRs in the area you're touching.\n\n## What a good test is\n\nTests verify behavior through public interfaces, not implementation details. Code can change entirely; tests shouldn't. A good test reads like a specification: \"user can checkout with valid cart\" tells you exactly what capability exists, and it survives refactors because it doesn't care about internal structure.\n\nSee [tests.md](tests.md) for examples and [mocking.md](mocking.md) for mocking guidelines.\n\n## Seams: where tests go\n\nA **seam** is the public boundary you test at: the interface where you observe behavior without reaching inside. Tests live at seams, never against internals.\n\n**Test only at pre-agreed seams.** Before writing any test, write down the seams under test and confirm them with the user. No test is written at an unconfirmed seam. You can't test everything, so agreeing the seams up front is how testing effort lands on the critical paths and complex logic instead of every edge case.\n\nAsk: \"What's the public interface, and which seams should we test?\"\n\nWhen the shape of that interface is itself in question (how deep the module is, where the seam belongs, what the interface should expose), call the Skill tool with \"codebase-design\" for the vocabulary. It is the shared source of the module, interface, depth, seam, adapter, leverage and locality terms, and it is a reference to consult, not a session to run.\n\n## Anti-patterns\n\n- **Implementation-coupled**: mocks internal collaborators, tests private methods, or verifies through a side channel (querying the database instead of using the interface). The tell: the test breaks when you refactor but behavior hasn't changed.\n- **Tautological**: the assertion recomputes the expected value the way the code does (`expect(add(a, b)).toBe(a + b)`, a snapshot derived by hand the same way, a constant asserted equal to itself), so it passes by construction and can never disagree with the code. Expected values must come from an independent source of truth: a known-good literal, a worked example, the spec.\n- **Horizontal slicing**: writing all tests first, then all implementation. Bulk tests verify _imagined_ behavior: you test the _shape_ of things rather than user-facing behavior, the tests go insensitive to real changes, and you commit to test structure before understanding the implementation. Work in **vertical slices** instead: one test → one implementation → repeat, each test a **tracer bullet** that responds to what the last cycle taught you.\n\n## Rules of the loop\n\n- **Red before green.** Write the failing test first, then only enough code to pass it. Don't anticipate future tests or add speculative features.\n- **One slice at a time.** One seam, one test, one minimal implementation per cycle.\n- **Refactoring is not part of the loop.** It belongs to the review stage (see the `code-review` skill), not the red → green implementation cycle.\n"}]},"isMeta":true,"turnCompanion":true,"uuid":"b23dd8c8-a98a-45e3-b8d9-75aa787166ea","timestamp":"2026-09-28T22:03:02.953Z","sourceToolUseID":"toolu_01SpnZdZfLxcMZx1AEqSTY5q","session_id":"fc138a42-1eee-4017-bff2-c6a77302f820","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/davelleat/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"fc138a42-1eee-4017-bff2-c6a77302f820","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

Data Bites (issue #4).\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport re\n\nfrom fastapi.testclient import TestClient\n\nfrom tests.conftest import post_form\n\nBITE = {\"title\": \"Fall enrollment snapshot\", \"slug\": \"fall-enrollment\",\n        \"body\": \"Enrollment is **1,745** this fall.\"}\n\n\ndef bite_row(c: TestClient, slug: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"This Data Bite's row of the admin list, as HTML.\"\"\"\n    for row in c.get(\"/admin/data-bites\").text.split(\"<tr\")[1:]:\n        if f\"<code>{slug}</code>\" in row:\n            return row\n    raise AssertionError(f\"{slug} is not listed\")\n\n\ndef bite_id(c: TestClient, slug: str) -> str:\n    return re.match(r' id=\"data-bite-(\\d+)\"', bite_row(c, slug)).group(1)\n\n\ndef create_bite(c: TestClient, **override) -> str:\n    response = post_form(c, \"/admin/data-bites\", {**BITE, **override})\n    assert response.status_code == 303, response.text\n    return bite_id(c, override.get(\"slug\", BITE[\"slug\"]))\n\n\ndef test_analyst_creates_a_data_bite_as_a_draft(client_as):\n    analyst = client_as(\"editor\")\n    response = post_form(analyst, \"/admin/data-bites\", BITE)\n    assert response.status_code == 303\n    assert response.headers[\"location\"] == \"/admin/data-bites\"\n\n    row = bite_row(analyst, BITE[\"slug\"])\n    assert BITE[\"title\"] in row and \"Draft\" in Every table's schema lives here, so init_db() builds them all.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\nfrom collections.abc import Iterator\nfrom contextlib import contextmanager\n\nfrom app import settings\n\nSCHEMA = \"\"\"\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users (\n    id            INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,\n    email         TEXT    NOT NULL UNIQUE COLLATE NOCASE,\n    name          TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    role          TEXT    NOT NULL CHECK (role IN ('admin', 'editor')),\n    password_hash TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    is_active     INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,\n    created_at    TEXT    NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now'))\n);\n\"\"\"\n\n\n@contextmanager\ndef connect() -> Iterator[sqlite3.Connection]:\n    \"\"\"Open the database named by settings (read at call time, so tests can swap\n    it). Commits on success, rolls back on error, always closes.\"\"\"\n    conn = sqlite3.connect(settings.DATABASE_PATH)\n    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n    conn.execute(\"PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON\")\n    try:\n        with conn:\n            yield conn\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n\n\ndef init_db() -> None:\n    with connect() as conn:\n        conn.executescript(SCHEMA)\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":17,"oldLines":6,"newStart":17,"newLines":17,"lines":["     is_active     INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,","     created_at    TEXT    NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now'))"," );","+","+CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS data_bites (","+    id         INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,","+    title      TEXT    NOT NULL,","+    slug       TEXT    NOT NULL UNIQUE,","+    body       TEXT    NOT NULL,  -- raw Markdown; sanitized only when rendered","+    status     TEXT    NOT NULL DEFAULT 'draft' CHECK (status IN ('draft', 'published')),","+    author_id  INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES users (id),","+    created_at TEXT    NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now')),","+    updated_at TEXT    NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now'))","+);"," \"\"\""," "," "]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false,"contentNotInModelContext":true},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"c297aeea-66ae-449c-884c-cae5f85f8daa","session_id":"fc138a42-1eee-4017-bff2-c6a77302f820","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/davelleat/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"fc138a42-1eee-4017-bff2-c6a77302f820","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"1fb3971a-7350-4272-87d1-14262c476b53","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-5-5","id":"msg_011CfWbNAiv8Kz7M26cSD91B","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_014ugKdAfNxsgdsdeu2Fse19","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/davelleat/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/app/data_bites.py","content":"\"\"\"Data Bites: short, dated Markdown items.\n\nAny Analyst or Director may create, edit, publish, and unpublish any Data Bite\n(shift continuity, ADR-003); only a Director may delete one, which the routes\nenforce. Editing never changes the author or created_at.\n\nPublic-facing code reads Data Bites only through the `*_published` functions,\nso a draft cannot leak through them.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport re\nimport sqlite3\n\nfrom app import db\n\nSTATUS_LABELS = {\"draft\": \"Draft\", \"published\": \"Published\"}\n_SLUG_PATTERN = re.compile(r\"[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*\")\n_SELECT = (\"SELECT d.*, u.name AS author_name FROM data_bites d\"\n           \" JOIN users u ON u.id = d.author_id\")\n\n\nclass DataBiteError(ValueError):\n    \"\"\"A Data Bite broke a rule; the message is safe to show.\"\"\"\n\n\nclass DuplicateSlug(DataBiteError):\n    pass\n\n\ndef _clean(title: str, slug: str, body: str) -> tuple[str, str, str]:\n    title, slug = title.strip(), slug.strip()\n    if not title:\n        raise DataBiteError(\"Enter a title.\")\n    if not _SLUG_PATTERN.fullmatch(slug):\n        raise DataBiteError(\"Slugs use lowercase letters, digits, and single \"\n                            \"hyphens, e.g. fall-enrollment.\")\n    if not body.strip():\n        raise DataBiteError(\"Enter a body.\")\n    return title, slug, body\n\n\ndef _duplicate(slug: str) -> DuplicateSlug:\n    return DuplicateSlug(f\"Another Data Bite already uses the slug {slug}.\")\n\n\ndef create(title: str, slug: str, body: str, author_id: int) -> int:\n    \"\"\"Create a draft Data Bite.\"\"\"\n    title, slug, body = _clean(title, slug, body)\n    try:\n        with db.connect() as conn:\n            cursor = conn.execute(\n                \"INSERT INTO data_bites (title, slug, body, author_id) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n                (title, slug, body, author_id),\n            )\n    except sqlite3.IntegrityError:\n        raise _duplicate(slug) from None\n    return cursor.lastrowid\n\n\ndef list_all() -> list[sqlite3.Row]:\n    with db.connect() as conn:\n        return conn.execute(f\"{_SELECT} ORDER BY d.updated_at DESC, d.id DESC\").fetchall()\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1759,"cache_read_input_tokens":74882,"output_tokens":3578,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":41},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1759,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":3578,"cache_read_input_tokens":74882,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1759,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1759},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"wireToolInputs":{"toolu_014ugKdAfNxsgdsdeu2Fse19":{"file_path":"/Users/davelleat/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/app/data_bites.py","content":"\"\"\"Data routes\nenforce. Editing never changes the author or created_at.\n\nPublic-facing code reads Data Bites only through the `*_published` functions,\nso a draft cannot leak through them.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport re\nimport sqlite3\n\nfrom app import db\n\nSTATUS_LABELS = {\"draft\": \"Draft\", \"published\": \"Published\"}\n_SLUG_PATTERN = re.compile(r\"[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*\")\n_SELECT = (\"SELECT d.*, u.name AS author_name FROM data_bites d\"\n           \" JOIN users u ON u.id = d.author_id\")\n\n\nclass DataBiteError(ValueError):\n    \"\"\"A Data Bite broke a rule; the message is safe to show.\"\"\"\n\n\nclass DuplicateSlug(DataBiteError):\n    pass\n\n\ndef _clean(title: str, slug: str, body: str) -> tuple[str, str, str]:\n    title, slug = title.strip(), slug.strip()\n    if not title:\n        raise DataBiteError(\"Enter a title.\")\n    if not _SLUG_PATTERN.fullmatch(slug):\n        raise DataBiteError(\"Slugs use lowercase letters, digits, and single \"\n                            \"hyphens, e.g. fall-enrollment.\")\n    if not body.strip():\n        raise DataBiteError(\"Enter a body.\")\n    return title, slug, body\n\n\ndef _duplicate(slug: str) -> DuplicateSlug:\n    return DuplicateSlug(f\"Another Data Bite already uses the slug {slug}.\")\n\n\ndef create(title: str, slug: str, body: str, author_id: int) -> int:\n    \"\"\"Create a draft Data Bite.\"\"\"\n    title, slug, body = _clean(title, slug, body)\n    try:\n        with db.connect() as conn:\n            cursor = conn.execute(\n                \"INSERT INTO data_bites (title, slug, body, author_id) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n                (title, slug, body, author_id),\n            )\n    except sqlite3.IntegrityError:\n        raise _duplicate(slug) from None\n    return cursor.lastrowid\n\n\ndef list_all() -> list[sqlite3.Row]:\n    with db.connect() as conn:\n        return conn.execute(f\"{_SELECT} ORDER BY d.updated_at DESC, d.id DESC\").fetchall()\n"}},"apiBlockIndex":3,"requestId":"req_011CfWbNAKN5nuxbYB6y4NC7","attributionSkill":"mattpocock-skills:tdd","attributionPlugin":"mattpocock-skills","type":"assistant","uuid":"f648679f-c6e8-434f-b775-802a53c9485e","timestamp":"2026-09-28T22:03:29.317Z","advisorModel":"claude-opus-5-5","effort":"high","perTurnEffort":"high","session_id":"fc138a42-1eee-4017-bff2-c6a77302f820","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/davelleat/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"fc138a42-1eee-4017-bff2-c6a77302f820","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

Editing never changes the author or created_at.\n\nPublic-facing code reads Data Bites only through the `*_published` functions,\nso a draft cannot leak through them.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport re\nimport sqlite3\n\nfrom app import db\n\nSTATUS_LABELS = {\"draft\": \"Draft\", \"published\": \"Published\"}\n_SLUG_PATTERN = re.compile(r\"[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*\")\n_SELECT = (\"SELECT d.*, u.name AS author_name FROM data_bites d\"\n           \" JOIN users u ON u.id = d.author_id\")\n\n\nclass DataBiteError(ValueError):\n    \"\"\"A Data Bite broke a rule; the message is safe to show.\"\"\"\n\n\nclass DuplicateSlug(DataBiteError):\n    pass\n\n\ndef _clean(title: str, slug: str, body: str) -> tuple[str, str, str]:\n    title, slug = title.strip(), slug.strip()\n    if not title:\n        raise DataBiteError(\"Enter a title.\")\n    if not _SLUG_PATTERN.fullmatch(slug):\n        raise DataBiteError(\"Slugs use lowercase letters, digits, and single \"\n                            \"hyphens, e.g. fall-enrollment.\")\n    if not body.strip():\n        raise DataBiteError(\"Enter a body.\")\n    return title, slug, body\n\n\ndef _duplicate(slug: str) -> DuplicateSlug:\n    return DuplicateSlug(f\"Another Data Bite already uses the slug {slug}.\")\n\n\ndef create(title: str, slug: str, body: str, author_id: int) -> int:\n    \"\"\"Create a draft Data Bite.\"\"\"\n    title, slug, body = _clean(title, slug, body)\n    try:\n        with db.connect() as conn:\n            cursor = conn.execute(\n                \"INSERT INTO data_bites (title, slug, body, author_id) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n                (title, slug, body, author_id),\n            )\n    except sqlite3.IntegrityError:\n        raise _duplicate(slug) from None\n    return cursor.lastrowid\n\n\ndef list_all() -> list[sqlite3.Row]:\n    with db.connect() as conn:\n        return conn.execute(f\"{_SELECT} ORDER BY d.updated_at DESC, d.id DESC\").fetchall()\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"f648679f-c6e8-434f-b775-802a53c9485e","session_id":"fc138a42-1eee-4017-bff2-c6a77302f820","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/davelleat/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"fc138a42-1eee-4017-bff2-c6a77302f820","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

create_bite(request: Request, user=Depends(current_user), title: str = Form(\"\"),\n                slug: str = Form(\"\"), body: str = Form(\"\")):\n    try:\n        data_bites.create(title, slug, Any signed-in user may do everything here\n2\texcept delete, which is Director only.\"\"\"\n3\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n4\t\n5\timport sqlite3\n6\t\n7\tfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Form, HTTPException, Request\n8\tfrom fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse\n9\t\n10\tfrom app import data_bites\n11\tfrom app.auth import current_user, require_csrf\n12\tfrom app.templating import templates\n13\t\n14\trouter = APIRouter(prefix=\"/admin/data-bites\", dependencies=[Depends(current_user)])\n15\t\n...\n34\tdef create_bite(request: Request, user=Depends(current_user), title: str = Form(\"\"),\n35\t                slug: str = Form(\"\"), body: str = Form(\"\")):\n36\t    try:\n37\t        data_bites.create(title, slug, body, user[\"id\"])\n38\t    except data_bites.DataBiteError as exc:\n39\t        return _list_page(request, user, error=str(exc), status_code=400,\n40\t                          form={\"title\": title, \"slug\": slug, \"body\": body})\n41\t    return RedirectResponse(\"/admin/data-bites\", status_code=303)\n42\t\n43\t\n44\tdef _set_status(bite_id: int, status: str):\n45\t    if not data_bites.set_status(bite_id, status):\n46\t        raise HTTPException(status_code=404)\n47\t    return RedirectResponse(\"/admin/data-bites\", status_code=303)\n48\t\n49\t\n50\t@router.post(\"/{bite_id}/publish\", dependencies=[Depends(require_csrf)])\n51\tdef publish(bite_id: int):\n52\t    return _set_status(bite_id, \"published\")\n53\t\n54\t\n55\t@router.post(\"/{bite_id}/unpublish\", dependencies=[Depends(require_csrf)])\n56\tdef unpublish(bite_id: int):\n57\t    return _set_status(bite_id, \"draft\")\n</system-reminder>"}],"session_id":"fc138a42-1eee-4017-bff2-c6a77302f820","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/davelleat/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"fc138a42-1eee-4017-bff2-c6a77302f820","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

- <<'EOF'\np='tests/test_t03_data_bites.py'; s=open(p).read()\ns=s.replace('''import re\n\nfrom fastapi.testclient import TestClient\n\nfrom tests.conftest import post_form\n''','''import re\n\nfrom fastapi.testclient import TestClient\n\nfrom app import db\nfrom app.main import create_app\nfrom tests.conftest import csrf_from, post_form\n''')\ns+='''\n\ndef second_analyst(director: TestClient) -> TestClient:\n    \"\"\"A logged-in client for a second, named Analyst (not the seeded one).\"\"\"\n    account = {\"name\": \"Riley Intern\", \"email\": \"riley@example.test\",\n               \"password\": \"a-long-enough-password\", \"role\": \"editor\"}\n    assert post_form(director, \"/admin/users\", account).status_code == 303\n    c = TestClient(create_app())\n    token = csrf_from(c.get(\"/login\").text)\n    c.post(\"/login\", data={**account, \"csrf_token\": token})\n    return c\n\n\ndef backdate(bite_id: str) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Setup only: move a Data Bite's timestamps into the past, so an edit made\n    within the same second still visibly changes updated_at.\"\"\"\n    with db.connect() as conn:\n        conn.execute(\"UPDATE data_bites SET created_at = '2026-01-05 09:00:00',\"\n                     \" updated_at = '2026-01-05 09:00:00' WHERE id = ?\", (bite_id,))\n\n\ndef test_any_analyst_edits_any_data_bite_and_author_and_created_at_are_kept(client_as):\n    riley = second_analyst(client_as(\"admin\"))\n    bid = create_bite(riley)\n    backdate(bid)\n\n    analyst = client_as(\"editor\")  # a different Analyst from the author\n    edit_page = analyst.get(f\"/admin/data-bites/{bid}\")\n    assert edit_page.status_code == 200 and BITE[\"body\"] in edit_page.text\n\n    edited = {\"title\": \"Fall enrollment (official)\", \"slug\": \"fall-enrollment-official\",\n              \"body\": \"Official count: **1,751**.\"}\n    response = post_form(analyst, f\"/admin/data-bites/{bid}\", edited)\n    assert response.status_code == 303\n\n    page = analyst.get(f\"/admin/data-bites/{bid}\").text\n    assert edited[\"title\"] in page and edited[\"body\"] in page\n    assert \"Created 2026-01-05 09:00:00 by Riley Intern\" in page\n    updated = re.search(r\"Last updated ([\\\\d:\\\\- ]+)\", page).group(1)\n    assert updated != \"2026-01-05 09:00:00\"\n    assert \"Riley Intern\" in bite_row(analyst, edited[\"slug\"])\n\n\ndef test_editing_to_another_data_bites_slug_is_rejected(client_as):\n    analyst = client_as(\"editor\")\n    create_bite(analyst)\n    other = create_bite(analyst, slug=\"spring-survey\", title=\"Spring survey\")\n    response = post_form(analyst, f\"/admin/data-bites/{other}\",\n                         {**BITE, \"slug\": BITE[\"slug\"], \"title\": \"Spring survey\"})\n    assert response.status_code == 400\n    assert f\"already uses the slug {BITE['slug']}\" in \"Riley Intern\", \"email\": \"riley@example.test\",\n               \"password\": \"a-long-enough-password\", \"role\": \"editor\"}\n    assert post_form(director, \"/admin/users\", account).status_code == 303\n    c = TestClient(create_app())\n    token = csrf_from(c.get(\"/login\").text)\n    c.post(\"/login\", data={**account, \"csrf_token\": token})\n    return c\n\n\ndef backdate(bite_id: str) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Setup only: move a Data Bite's timestamps into the past, so an edit made\n    within the same second still visibly changes updated_at.\"\"\"\n    with db.connect() as conn:\n        conn.execute(\"UPDATE data_bites SET created_at = '2026-01-05 09:00:00',\"\n                     \" updated_at = '2026-01-05 09:00:00' WHERE id = ?\", (bite_id,))\n\n\ndef test_any_analyst_edits_any_data_bite_and_author_and_created_at_are_kept(client_as):\n    riley = second_analyst(client_as(\"admin\"))\n    bid = create_bite(riley)\n    backdate(bid)\n\n    analyst = client_as(\"editor\")  # a different Analyst from the author\n    edit_page = analyst.get(f\"/admin/data-bites/{bid}\")\n    assert edit_page.status_code == 200 and BITE[\"body\"] in edit_page.text\n\n    edited = {\"title\": \"Fall enrollment (official)\", \"slug\": \"fall-enrollment-official\",\n              \"body\": \"Official count: **1,751**.\"}\n    response = post_form(analyst, f\"/admin/data-bites/{bid}\", edited)\n    assert response.status_code == 303\n\n    page = analyst.get(f\"/admin/data-bites/{bid}\").text\n    assert edited[\"title\"] in page and edited[\"body\"] in page\n    assert \"Created 2026-01-05 09:00:00 by Riley Intern\" in page\n    updated = re.search(r\"Last updated ([\\\\d:\\\\- ]+)\", page).group(1)\n    assert updated != \"2026-01-05 09:00:00\"\n    assert \"Riley Intern\" in bite_row(analyst, edited[\"slug\"])\n\n\ndef test_editing_to_another_data_bites_slug_is_rejected(client_as):\n    analyst = client_as(\"editor\")\n    create_bite(analyst)\n    other = create_bite(analyst, slug=\"spring-survey\", title=\"Spring survey\")\n    response = post_form(analyst, f\"/admin/data-bites/{other}\",\n                         {**BITE, \"slug\": BITE[\"slug\"], \"title\": \"Spring survey\"})\n    assert response.status_code == 400\n    assert f\"already uses the slug {BITE['slug']}\" in response.text\n    assert bite_id(analyst, \"spring-survey\") == other\n'''\nopen(p,'w').write(s)\nEOF\nuv run pytest tests/test_t03_data_bites.py -q 2>&1 | tail -4","description":"Add edit tests The author, created_at, and status are kept.\n    False if there is no such Data Bite.\"\"\"\n    title, slug, body = _clean(title, slug, body)\n    try:\n        with db.connect() as conn:\n            return conn.execute(\n                \"UPDATE data_bites SET title = ?, slug = ?, body = ?,\"\n                \" updated_at = datetime('now') WHERE id = ?\",\n                (title, slug, body, bite_id),\n            ).rowcount == 1\n    except sqlite3.IntegrityError:\n        raise _duplicate(slug) from None\n\n\ndef get(bite_id: int) -> sqlite3.Row | None:\n    with db.connect() as conn:\n        return conn.execute(f\"{_SELECT} WHERE d.id = ?\", (bite_id,)).fetchone()\n\n\ndef list_all()''')\nopen(p,'w').write(s)\n\np='app/routes/data_bites.py'; s=open(p).read()\ns=s.replace('''\n\ndef _set_status(''','''\n\ndef _edit_page(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row, bite: sqlite3.Row, *,\n               error: str | None = None, form: dict | None = None,\n               status_code: int = 200):\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/data_bite_edit.html\",\n        {\"title\": \"Edit Data Bite\", \"home_path\": \"/admin\", \"user\": user,\n         \"bite\": bite, \"status_labels\": data_bites.STATUS_LABELS,\n         \"error\": error, \"form\": form or bite},\n        status_code=status_code,\n    )\n\n\ndef _get_or_404(bite_id: int) -> sqlite3.Row:\n    bite = data_bites.get(bite_id)\n    if bite is None:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=404)\n    return bite\n\n\n@router.get(\"/{bite_id}\")\ndef edit_form(request: Request, bite_id: int, user=Depends(current_user)):\n    return _edit_page(request, user, _get_or_404(bite_id))\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{bite_id}\", dependencies=[Depends(require_csrf)])\ndef edit_bite(request: Request, bite_id: int, user=Depends(current_user),\n              title: str = Form(\"\"), slug: str = Form(\"\"), body: str = Form(\"\")):\n    bite = _get_or_404(bite_id)\n    try:\n        found = data_bites.update(bite_id, title, slug, body)\n    except data_bites.DataBiteError as exc:\n        return _edit_page(request, user, bite, error=str(exc), status_code=400,\n                          form={\"title\": title, \"slug\": slug, \"body\": body})\n    if not found:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=404)\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/data-bites/{bite_id}\", status_code=303)\n\n\ndef _set_status(''')\nopen(p,'w').write(s)\nEOF\ncat > templates/admin/data_bite_edit.html <<'EOF'\n{% extends \"base.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n  <p><a href=\"/admin/data-bites\">All Data Bites</a></p>\n  <h1>Edit Data Bite</h1>\n  <p>{{ status_labels[bite.status] }}. {"parentUuid":"8a53471e-1fa9-4aa6-9443-c4cdc20d70ab","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-5-5","id":"msg_011CfWbVzXUJmFRCR6yh4REP","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01Jdxz7M8N6piyfw31HxHprf","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"python3 - <<'EOF'\np='tests/test_t03_data_bites.py'; s=open(p).read()\ns=s.replace(\"import re\\n\\nfrom fastapi\",\"import re\\n\\nimport pytest\\nfrom fastapi\")\ns+='''\n\n# Every Data Bite route. {id} is filled with an existing Data Bite's id.\nROUTES = [\n    (\"GET\", \"/admin/data-bites\", {}),\n    (\"POST\", \"/admin/data-bites\", {**BITE, \"slug\": \"new-slug\"}),\n    (\"GET\", \"/admin/data-bites/{id}\", {}),\n    (\"POST\", \"/admin/data-bites/{id}\", {**BITE, \"title\": \"Changed title\"}),\n    (\"POST\", \"/admin/data-bites/{id}/publish\", {}),\n    (\"POST\", \"/admin/data-bites/{id}/unpublish\", {}),\n    (\"POST\", \"/admin/data-bites/{id}/delete\", {}),\n]\nPOST_ROUTES = [r for r in ROUTES if r[0] == \"POST\"]\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef existing_bite(client_as):\n    return create_bite(client_as(\"editor\"))\n\n\ndef assert_unchanged(c: TestClient):\n    \"\"\"The Data Bite from `existing_bite` is still the only one, still a draft,\n    with its original title.\"\"\"\n    listing = c.get(\"/admin/data-bites\").text\n    assert listing.count('id=\"data-bite-') == 1\n    row = bite_row(c, BITE[\"slug\"])\n    assert BITE[\"title\"] in row and \"Draft\" in row\n\n\n@pytest.mark.parametrize(\"method, path, data\", ROUTES)\ndef test_anonymous_visitor_is_redirected_from_every_data_bite_route(\n        client, client_as, existing_bite, method, path, data):\n    response = client.request(method, path.format(id=existing_bite),\n                              data=data or None, follow_redirects=False)\n    assert response.status_code in (302, 303, 307)\n    assert response.headers[\"location\"].endswith(\"/login\")\n    assert_unchanged(client_as(\"admin\"))\n\n\n@pytest.mark.parametrize(\"method, path, data\", POST_ROUTES)\ndef test_data_bite_posts_without_csrf_token_are_rejected(\n        client_as, existing_bite, method, path, data):\n    director = client_as(\"admin\")\n    director.get(\"/admin\")  # the session has a token; the POST just omits it\n    response = post_form(director, path.format(id=existing_bite), data, with_csrf=False)\n    assert response.status_code == 403\n    assert_unchanged(director)\n\n\ndef test_every_signed_in_user_sees_the_data_bites_link(client_as):\n    for role in (\"admin\", \"editor\"):\n        assert 'href=\"/admin/data-bites\"' in client_as(role).get(\"/admin\").text\n\n\ndef test_missing_data_bite_is_404(client_as):\n    analyst = client_as(\"editor\")\n    assert analyst.get(\"/admin/data-bites/999\").status_code == 404\n    assert post_form(analyst, \"/admin/data-bites/999/publish\", {}).status_code == 404\n    assert post_form(analyst, \"/admin/data-bites/999\", BITE).status_code == 404\n'''\nopen(p,'w').write(s)\nEOF\nuv run pytest tests/test_t03_data_bites.py -q 2>&1 | tail -4","description":"Add access-control and CSRF tests"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":678,"cache_read_input_tokens":90938,"output_tokens":1346,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":90},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":678,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":1346,"cache_read_input_tokens":90938,"cache_creation_input_tokens":678,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":678},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"wireToolInputs":{"toolu_01Jdxz7M8N6piyfw31HxHprf":{"command":"cd /Users/davelleat/code/iphs400-mp2-cms && python3 - <<'EOF'\np='tests/test_t03_data_bites.py'; s=open(p).read()\ns=s.replace(\"import re\\n\\nfrom fastapi\",\"import re\\n\\nimport pytest\\nfrom fastapi\")\ns+='''\n\n# Every Data Bite route. {id} is filled with an existing Data Bite's id.\nROUTES = [\n    (\"GET\", \"/admin/data-bites\", {}),\n    (\"POST\", \"/admin/data-bites\", {**BITE, \"slug\": \"new-slug\"}),\n    (\"GET\", \"/admin/data-bites/{id}\", {}),\n    (\"POST\", \"/admin/data-bites/{id}\", {**BITE, \"title\": \"Changed title\"}),\n    (\"POST\", \"/admin/data-bites/{id}/publish\", {}),\n    (\"POST\", \"/admin/data-bites/{id}/unpublish\", {}),\n    (\"POST\", \"/admin/data-bites/{id}/delete\", {}),\n]\nPOST_ROUTES = [r for r in ROUTES if r[0] == \"POST\"]\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef existing_bite(client_as):\n    return create_bite(client_as(\"editor\"))\n\n\ndef assert_unchanged(c: TestClient):\n    \"\"\"The Data Bite from `existing_bite` is still the only one, still a draft,\n    with its original title.\"\"\"\n    listing = c.get(\"/admin/data-bites\").text\n    assert listing.count('id=\"data-bite-') == 1\n    row = bite_row(c, BITE[\"slug\"])\n    assert BITE[\"title\"] in row and \"Draft\" in row\n\n\n@pytest.mark.parametrize(\"method, path, data\", ROUTES)\ndef test_anonymous_visitor_is_redirected_from_every_data_bite_route(\n        client, client_as, existing_bite, method, path, data):\n    response = client.request(method, path.format(id=existing_bite),\n                              data=data or None, follow_redirects=False)\n    assert response.status_code in (302, 303, 307)\n    assert response.headers[\"location\"].endswith(\"/login\")\n    assert_unchanged(client_as(\"admin\"))\n\n\n@pytest.mark.parametrize(\"method, path, data\", POST_ROUTES)\ndef test_data_bite_posts_without_csrf_token_are_rejected(\n        client_as, existing_bite, method, path, data):\n    director = client_as(\"admin\")\n    director.get(\"/admin\")  # the session has a token; the POST just omits it\n    response = post_form(director, path.format(id=existing_bite), data, with_csrf=False)\n    assert response.status_code == 403\n    assert_unchanged(director)\n\n\ndef test_every_signed_in_user_sees_the_data_bites_link(client_as):\n    for role in (\"admin\", \"editor\"):\n        assert 'href=\"/admin/data-bites\"' in client_as(role).get(\"/admin\").text\n\n\ndef test_missing_data_bite_is_404(client_as):\n    analyst = client_as(\"editor\")\n    assert analyst.get(\"/admin/data-bites/999\").status_code == 404\n    assert post_form(analyst, \"/admin/data-bites/999/publish\", {}).status_code == 404\n    assert post_form(analyst, \"/admin/data-bites/999\", BITE).status_code == 404\n'''\nopen(p,'w').write(s)\nEOF\nuv run pytest tests/test_t03_data_bites.py -q 2>&1 | tail -4","description":"Add access-control and CSRF tests"}},"wireIngestContext":{"toolu_01Jdxz7M8N6piyfw31HxHprf":{"cwd":"/Users/davelleat/code/iphs400-mp2-cms"}},"apiBlockIndex":2,"requestId":"req_011CfWbVz3TJof2Vny6ZkChw","attributionSkill":"mattpocock-skills:tdd","attributionPlugin":"mattpocock-skills","type":"assistant","uuid":"ece269af-3370-4600-8236-87689033a817","timestamp":"2026-09-28T22:05:14.525Z","advisorModel":"claude-opus-5-5","effort":"high","perTurnEffort":"high","session_id":"fc138a42-1eee-4017-bff2-c6a77302f820","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/davelleat/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"fc138a42-1eee-4017-bff2-c6a77302f820","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"6fbdc5ba-bc6a-4cc4-a807-d11d46c0c709","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"fe935dab-cbcb-458c-aa72-102088c73805","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Base directory for this skill: /Users/davelleat/.claude/plugins/cache/claude-plugins-official/mattpocock-skills/1.2.3/skills/engineering/code-review\n\nTwo-axis review of the diff between `HEAD` and a fixed point the user supplies:\n\n- **Standards**: does the code conform to this repo's documented coding standards?\n- **Spec**: does the code faithfully implement the originating issue / spec?\n\nBoth axes run as **parallel sub-agents** so they don't pollute each other's context, then this skill aggregates their findings.\n\nThe issue tracker should have been provided to you. If `docs/agents/issue-tracker.md` is missing, tell the user to run `/setup-matt-pocock-skills`.\n\n## Process\n\n### 1. Pin the fixed point\n\nWhatever the user said is the fixed point (a commit SHA, branch name, tag, `main`, `HEAD~5`, etc.). If they didn't specify one, ask for it.\n\nCapture the diff command once: `git diff <fixed-point>...HEAD` (three-dot, so the comparison is against the merge-base). Also note the list of commits via `git log <fixed-point>..HEAD --oneline`.\n\nBefore going further, confirm the fixed point resolves (`git rev-parse <fixed-point>`) and the diff is non-empty. A bad ref or empty diff should fail here, not inside two parallel sub-agents.\n\n### 2. Identify the spec source\n\nLook for the originating spec, in this order:\n\n1. Issue references in the commit messages (`#123`, `Closes #45`, GitLab `!67`, etc.), fetched via the workflow in `docs/agents/issue-tracker.md`.\n2. A path the user passed as an argument.\n3. A spec file under `docs/`, `specs/`, or `.scratch/` matching the branch name or feature.\n4. If nothing is found, ask the user where the spec is. If they say there isn't one, the **Spec** sub-agent will skip and report \"no spec available\".\n\n### 3. Identify the standards sources\n\nAnything in the repo that documents how code should be written, such as `CODING_STANDARDS.md` or `CONTRIBUTING.md`.\n\nOn top of whatever the repo documents, the Standards axis always carries the **smell baseline** below: a fixed set of Fowler code smells (_Refactoring_, ch.3) that applies even when a repo documents nothing. Two rules bind it:\n\n- **The repo overrides.** A documented repo standard always wins; where it endorses something the baseline would flag, suppress the smell.\n- **Always a judgement call.** Each smell is a labelled heuristic (\"possible Feature Envy\"), never a hard violation. Like any standard here, skip anything tooling already enforces.\n\nEach smell reads *what it is* → *how to fix*; match it against the diff:\n\n- **Mysterious Name**: a function, variable, or type whose name doesn't reveal what it does or holds. → rename it; if no honest name comes, the design's murky.\n- **Duplicated Code**: the same logic shape appears in more than one hunk or file in the change. → extract the shared shape, call it from both.\n- **Feature Envy**: a method that reaches into another object's data more than its own. → move the method onto the data it envies.\n- **Data Clumps**: the same few fields or params keep travelling together (a type wanting to be born). → bundle them into one type, pass that.\n- **Primitive Obsession**: a primitive or string standing in for a domain concept that deserves its own type. → give the concept its own small type.\n- **Repeated Switches**: the same `switch`/`if`-cascade on the same type recurs across the change. → replace with polymorphism, or one map both sites share.\n- **Shotgun Surgery**: one logical change forces scattered edits across many files in the diff. → gather what changes together into one module.\n- **Divergent Change**: one file or module is edited for several unrelated reasons. → split so each module changes for one reason.\n- **Speculative Generality**: abstraction, parameters, or hooks added for needs the spec doesn't have. → delete it; inline back until a real need shows.\n- **Message Chains**: long `a.b().c().d()` navigation the caller shouldn't depend on. → hide the walk behind one method on the first object.\n- **Middle Man**: a class or function that mostly just delegates onward. → cut it, call the real target direct.\n- **Refused Bequest**: a subclass or implementer that ignores or overrides most of what it inherits. → drop the inheritance, use composition.\n\n### 4. Spawn both sub-agents in parallel\n\n**Standards sub-agent prompt** should include:\n\n- The full diff command and commit list.\n- The list of standards-source files you found in step 3, **plus the smell baseline from step 3** pasted in full (the sub-agent has no other access to it).\n- The brief: \"Report, per file/hunk where relevant, (a) every place the diff violates a documented standard: cite the standard (file + the rule); and (b) any baseline smell you spot: name it and quote the hunk. Distinguish hard violations from judgement calls: documented-standard breaches can be hard, but baseline smells are always judgement calls, and a documented repo standard overrides the baseline. Skip anything tooling enforces. Under 400 words.\"\n\n**Spec sub-agent prompt** should include:\n\n- The diff command and commit list.\n- The path or fetched contents of the spec.\n- The brief: \"Report: (a) requirements the spec asked for that are missing or partial; (b) behaviour in the diff that wasn't asked for (scope creep); (c) requirements that look implemented but where the implementation looks wrong. Quote the spec line for each finding. Under 400 words.\"\n\nIf the spec is missing, skip the Spec sub-agent and note this in the final report.\n\n### 5. Aggregate\n\nPresent the two reports under `## Standards` and `## Spec` headings, verbatim or lightly cleaned. Do **not** merge or rerank findings, because the two axes are deliberately separate (see _Why two axes_).\n\nEnd with a one-line summary: total findings per axis, and the worst issue _within each axis_ (if any). Don't pick a single winner across axes: that's the reranking the separation exists to prevent.\n\n## Why two axes\n\nA change can pass one axis and fail the other:\n\n- Code that follows every standard but implements the wrong thing → **Standards pass, Spec fail.**\n- Code that does exactly what the issue asked but breaks the project's conventions → **Spec pass, Standards fail.**\n\nReporting them separately stops one axis from masking the other.\n\n\nARGUMENTS: Review uncommitted working-tree changes against HEAD (b9fcf90) for ticket T03, GitHub issue #4 (spec #1). Files: app/data_bites.py, app/routes/data_bites.py, app/db.py, app/main.py, templates/admin/data_bites.html, templates/admin/_data_bite_fields.html, templates/admin/data_bite_edit.html, templates/admin/home.html, tests/test_t03_data_bites.py. Ignore notes/usage-ledger.csv and docs/transcripts."}]},"isMeta":true,"turnCompanion":true,"uuid":"be38f570-d88c-4b33-879a-439261615c45","timestamp":"2026-09-28T22:06:19.559Z","sourceToolUseID":"toolu_01Xii8q6yTXeU1Ay6AeUC197","session_id":"fc138a42-1eee-4017-bff2-c6a77302f820","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/davelleat/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"fc138a42-1eee-4017-bff2-c6a77302f820","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

The user can send it another message and resume it, so the same task-id may notify more than once.</note>\n<result>I found no hard violations of the repo's documented standards in the T03 diff. The smells below are all judgement calls.\n\n**Documented standards: all met**\n- **CSRF (CLAUDE.md, SECURITY-CHECKLIST #3):** every new POST route has `Depends(require_csrf)`, and every form includes the `csrf_token` hidden field. Tests cover both.\n- **Access (#4, #5, #10):** delete is restricted in code with `Depends(require_director)`, not just by hiding the button. The router is gated on `current_user`.\n- **Relative paths:** the public home uses `f\"data-bites/{b['slug']}.html\"`, which is relative. The `/admin/...` absolute links are in admin templates, which are never published, so the rule doesn't apply. T02 does the same.\n- **Drafts never leave the DB:** `list_published` filters on `status = 'published'`, and a test covers it.\n- **Markdown:** the body is only echoed, auto-escaped, inside a `&lt;textarea&gt;`. Nothing is rendered yet, so nothing needs sanitizing yet. #7 will apply once preview or export exists.\n- **Secrets, dependencies, CSS:** `cms.db` is gitignored. No new dependencies or CSS classes were added.\n- **Vocabulary (CONTEXT.md):** a minor call. CONTEXT says to avoid \"Unpublished\" for a Draft. `/unpublish` and the \"Unpublish\" button use it as a verb, which the grading rubric also does, so I'd call it acceptable. The code's `admin`/`editor` role strings are an existing convention that `users.py` maps to Director/Analyst.\n- **Doc inaccuracy (not a rule breach):** the `app/data_bites.py` docstring mentions \"the `*_published` functions\", but only `list_published` exists.\n\n**Possible smells (all judgement calls)**\n- **Possible Data Clumps:** `title, slug, body` travel together through `_clean`, `create`, `update`, both route signatures, and the form dict `{\"title\": title, \"slug\": slug, \"body\": body}`, which appears twice in the routes.\n- **Possible Primitive Obsession:** the status strings `\"draft\"`/`\"published\"` appear in several places. These are the SQL literal in `list_published`, the template's `b.status == \"published\"`, and `_set_status(bite_id, \"published\"/\"draft\")`.\n- **Possible Duplicated Code:** `_list_page` and `_edit_page` repeat the shape of `_users_page`. The `if not ...: raise HTTPException(status_code=404)` pattern appears 4 times. `edit_bite` does `_get_or_404(bite_id)` and then checks `if not found: raise HTTPException(404)` again, so one of the two checks is redundant.\n- **Possible Feature Envy:** `main.py` `public_home` builds `href: f\"data-bites/{slug}.html\"`. That knowledge of the export path belongs in `app/data_bites.py` or the future publish module, otherwise T07 will have to edit it in two places.\n- **Possible Speculative Generality:** `set_status` checks the status against `STATUS_LABELS`, but it is only ever called with hard-coded values.\n- **Possible Mysterious Name:** `_clean` is actually validate-and-normalise, and `_duplicate` builds an exception. `b` and `bid` are terse but local, so they're minor.\n\nThe only file I read is the diff at `/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-davelleat-code-iphs400-mp2-cms/fc138a42-1eee-4017-bff2-c6a77302f820/scratchpad/t03.diff`. The user can send it another message and resume it, so the same task-id may notify more than once.</note>\n<result>I found no hard violations of the repo's documented standards in the T03 diff. The smells below are all judgement calls.\n\n**Documented standards: all met**\n- **CSRF (CLAUDE.md, SECURITY-CHECKLIST #3):** every new POST route has `Depends(require_csrf)`, and every form includes the `csrf_token` hidden field. Tests cover both.\n- **Access (#4, #5, #10):** delete is restricted in code with `Depends(require_director)`, not just by hiding the button. The router is gated on `current_user`.\n- **Relative paths:** the public home uses `f\"data-bites/{b['slug']}.html\"`, which is relative. The `/admin/...` absolute links are in admin templates, which are never published, so the rule doesn't apply. T02 does the same.\n- **Drafts never leave the DB:** `list_published` filters on `status = 'published'`, and a test covers it.\n- **Markdown:** the body is only echoed, auto-escaped, inside a `&lt;textarea&gt;`. Nothing is rendered yet, so nothing needs sanitizing yet. #7 will apply once preview or export exists.\n- **Secrets, dependencies, CSS:** `cms.db` is gitignored. No new dependencies or CSS classes were added.\n- **Vocabulary (CONTEXT.md):** a minor call. CONTEXT says to avoid \"Unpublished\" for a Draft. `/unpublish` and the \"Unpublish\" button use it as a verb, which the grading rubric also does, so I'd call it acceptable. The code's `admin`/`editor` role strings are an existing convention that `users.py` maps to Director/Analyst.\n- **Doc inaccuracy (not a rule breach):** the `app/data_bites.py` docstring mentions \"the `*_published` functions\", but only `list_published` exists.\n\n**Possible smells (all judgement calls)**\n- **Possible Data Clumps:** `title, slug, body` travel together through `_clean`, `create`, `update`, both route signatures, and the form dict `{\"title\": title, \"slug\": slug, \"body\": body}`, which appears twice in the routes.\n- **Possible Primitive Obsession:** the status strings `\"draft\"`/`\"published\"` appear in several places. These are the SQL literal in `list_published`, the template's `b.status == \"published\"`, and `_set_status(bite_id, \"published\"/\"draft\")`.\n- **Possible Duplicated Code:** `_list_page` and `_edit_page` repeat the shape of `_users_page`. The `if not ...: raise HTTPException(status_code=404)` pattern appears 4 times. `edit_bite` does `_get_or_404(bite_id)` and then checks `if not found: raise HTTPException(404)` again, so one of the two checks is redundant.\n- **Possible Feature Envy:** `main.py` `public_home` builds `href: f\"data-bites/{slug}.html\"`. That knowledge of the export path belongs in `app/data_bites.py` or the future publish module, otherwise T07 will have to edit it in two places.\n- **Possible Speculative Generality:** `set_status` checks the status against `STATUS_LABELS`, but it is only ever called with hard-coded values.\n- **Possible Mysterious Name:** `_clean` is actually validate-and-normalise, and `_duplicate` builds an exception. `b` and `bid` are terse but local, so they're minor.\n\nThe only file I read is the diff at `/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-davelleat-code-iphs400-mp2-cms/fc138a42-1eee-4017-bff2-c6a77302f820/scratchpad/t03.diff`. {"type":"queue-operation","operation":"enqueue","timestamp":"2026-09-28T22:07:29.420Z","sessionId":"fc138a42-1eee-4017-bff2-c6a77302f820","content":"<task-notification>\n<task-id>ac8e8006074ad8d53</task-id>\n<tool-use-id>toolu_01XR9k6tgS1vCJGWmVkiNHw5</tool-use-id>\n<output-file>/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-davelleat-code-iphs400-mp2-cms/fc138a42-1eee-4017-bff2-c6a77302f820/tasks/ac8e8006074ad8d53.output</output-file>\n<status>completed</status>\n<summary>Agent \"Spec review of T03 diff\" finished</summary>\n<note>A task-notification fires each time this agent stops with no live background children of its own. The user can send it another message and resume it, so the same task-id may notify more than once.</note>\n<result>I found no missing requirements, but one piece of scope creep that conflicts with T07/T08, plus two minor implementation issues. All 22 tests in `tests/test_t03_data_bites.py` pass.\n\n**(a) Missing or partial**\n\nNothing is missing. Each acceptance criterion in #4 maps to code and a test: create as draft, publish/unpublish, Analyst delete 403 vs Director delete, duplicate slug 400 on create and on edit, author and created_at kept when a second named Analyst edits, drafts absent from `/`, CSRF on all 5 POST routes, and anonymous users redirected on all 7 routes.\n\nOne gap in the evidence: the draft-leak test only checks the live `/` route. The spec's `site/` draft-leak test belongs to T08, so this is acceptable. Just note that \"never returned by any public-facing query\" is only as strong as \"public code only calls `list_published()`\", and nothing enforces that.\n\n**(b) Scope creep / pre-empting other tickets**\n\n1. **`public_home` in `app/main.py` now lists published Data Bites** with links to `data-bites/&lt;slug&gt;.html`. Spec: *\"Other tickets own: public Data Bite pages (T07), `cms publish` export (T08)\"*.\n   - On the dev server those links 404, because nothing serves `data-bites/*.html` until T07.\n   - `app/publish.py` still renders `index.html` with `items=[]`, so the live `/` and the exported site already disagree.\n   - Either drop this and test draft-exclusion through `data_bites.list_published()` directly, or accept it and note the tie-in for T07/T08.\n2. **\"Unpublish\" in the UI.** CONTEXT.md, under Draft: *\"_Avoid_: Unpublished, pending\"*. The button and route are \"unpublish\", and the spec itself uses that verb, so this probably doesn't matter. The resulting status is correctly labelled \"Draft\".\n\n**(c) Implemented, but possibly wrong**\n\n1. **Every `sqlite3.IntegrityError` is reported as a duplicate slug** (`app/data_bites.py`, `create`/`update`). Spec: *\"two items of the same type sharing a slug is rejected at save time with a form validation error\"*. Slug collisions are handled correctly. But a foreign-key failure on `author_id` (if FKs are enforced) or any future constraint would also show \"Another Data Bite already uses the slug…\", which is misleading. It's low risk today, since title and body are checked non-empty first.\n2. **Publish and unpublish bump `updated_at`** (`set_status`). Spec: *\"the original author and `created_at` are preserved, only `updated_at` changes\"*. This is consistent with the spec, just flagging it as a deliberate reading: a status change counts as an edit. Author and `created_at` are never written after insert, which is correct.\n\n**Spec inconsistency (not the diff's fault)**\n\n`spec.md`'s Out of Scope still lists *\"per-intern individual accounts (deliberately rejected, see ADR-003)\"*. Implementation Decisions says ADR-003 reversed that. The diff and tests follow the reversal (named second Analyst \"Riley Intern\"), which matches CONTEXT.md. The spec issue should be corrected.\n\nFiles reviewed: `/Users/davelleat/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/app/data_bites.py`, `/Users/davelleat/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/app/routes/data_bites.py`, `/Users/davelleat/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/app/main.py`, `/Users/davelleat/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/app/publish.py`, `/Users/davelleat/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/tests/test_t03_data_bites.py`.</result>\n<usage><subagent_tokens>54814</subagent_tokens><tool_uses>5</tool_uses><duration_ms>44574</duration_ms></usage>\n</task-notification>"}

{"parentUuid":"451164b8-20f4-4598-9f87-c9f5b2ed3ce4","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"53d455c3-b7c7-4067-a871-08a8b20638a3","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01HAVAVk6DLBC71M72qrHtZy","type":"tool_result","content":"base=<unset>\ndocs: glossary, ADR-003, client brief, field notes, usage ledger, transcripts\n\n- CONTEXT.md: IR office glossary (Director, Analyst, Data Bite, Report,\n  Draft, Published, Factbook, CDS, Survey Calendar)\n- docs/adr/ADR-003: split publish rights; individual Analyst accounts\n- notes/: client brief, WordPress Playground field notes, usage ledger\n- docs/transcripts/: chat sessions 01-03\n\nCo-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>\n---\nT02: User management — create, change role, deactivate\n\n- /admin/users (Director only): list users with Director/Analyst labels,\n  add a user, change a user's role, deactivate a user. Rows are kept on\n  deactivation so content stays attributed; the user's open sessions end on\n  their next request.\n- require_director dependency in app/auth.py guards the whole router, so an\n  Analyst gets 403 on every route; anonymous visitors still go to /login.\n  Every POST carries a CSRF token.\n- users.create_user validates email, name and password length (>= 8) and\n  raises UserError / DuplicateEmail with messages the form shows (was a 500).\n- A Director can't demote or deactivate their own account, so the office\n  always keeps an active Director; the UI shows no controls on that row.\n- Admin home links to user management for Directors only.\n- seed_demo.py reports a too-short .env password instead of crashing.\n- tests: test_t02_users.py; shared post_form/user_row/user_id helpers in\n  conftest; T01's deactivation tests now go through the UI.\n\nCloses #3","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"8a9e2255-bb58-4c6a-b105-cff3006f1e19","timestamp":"2026-09-28T22:08:07.775Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"base=<unset>\ndocs: glossary, ADR-003, client brief, field notes, usage ledger, transcripts\n\n- CONTEXT.md: IR office glossary (Director, Analyst, Data Bite, Report,\n  Draft, Published, Factbook, CDS, Survey Calendar)\n- docs/adr/ADR-003: split publish rights; individual Analyst accounts\n- notes/: client brief, WordPress Playground field notes, usage ledger\n- docs/transcripts/: chat sessions 01-03\n\nCo-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>\n---\nT02: User management — create, change role, deactivate\n\n- /admin/users (Director only): list users with Director/Analyst labels,\n  add a user, change a user's role, deactivate a user. Rows are kept on\n  deactivation so content stays attributed; the user's open sessions end on\n  their next request.\n- require_director dependency in app/auth.py guards the whole router, so an\n  Analyst gets 403 on every route; anonymous visitors still go to /login.\n  Every POST carries a CSRF token.\n- users.create_user validates email, name and password length (>= 8) and\n  raises UserError / DuplicateEmail with messages the form shows (was a 500).\n- A Director can't demote or deactivate their own account, so the office\n  always keeps an active Director; the UI shows no controls on that row.\n- Admin home links to user management for Directors only.\n- seed_demo.py reports a too-short .env password instead of crashing.\n- tests: test_t02_users.py; shared post_form/user_row/user_id helpers in\n  conftest; T01's deactivation tests now go through the UI.\n\nCloses #3","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"451164b8-20f4-4598-9f87-c9f5b2ed3ce4","session_id":"fc138a42-1eee-4017-bff2-c6a77302f820","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/davelleat/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"fc138a42-1eee-4017-bff2-c6a77302f820","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"92a2c05a-40ed-4325-b5c5-4194c8fb7233","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-5-5","id":"msg_011CfWbjXJWrbV9qMLYq96Sq","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01DvQfdxcmucDF1PS3aTRbnb","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"gh issue comment 4 --body \"$(cat <<'EOF'\n## /code-review findings for T03 and how each was resolved\n\nTwo-axis review (Standards and Spec) of the T03 diff against `b9fcf90`.\n\n### Standards: no hard violations\nCSRF is on every new POST. Delete is Director-only in code (`require_director`), not just a hidden button. The public list is relative-path only and published-only. No new dependencies.\n\n| Finding | Resolution |\n|---|---|\n| Module docstring mentions \"`*_published` functions\" but only `list_published` exists | **Fixed**: the docstring now names `list_published` |\n| Possible Speculative Generality: `set_status` re-validates status that is only ever passed as a literal (the DB `CHECK` enforces it anyway) | **Fixed**: check removed |\n| Possible Duplicated Code: `edit_bite` checked for 404 twice (`_get_or_404` then `if not found`) | **Fixed**: the redundant second check is removed |\n| Possible Mysterious Name: `_clean`, `_duplicate` | **Fixed**: renamed to `_validate` and `_duplicate_slug_error` |\n| Possible Feature Envy: `main.py` built the `data-bites/<slug>.html` href | **Fixed**: the href was removed (see the Spec finding below) |\n| Possible Data Clumps (`title, slug, body`) and page-helper shape repeated from `_users_page` | **Kept**: matches the T02 convention. Revisit if T04 Reports repeat it a third time |\n| Possible Primitive Obsession: `\"draft\"`/`\"published\"` strings | **Kept**: two values, enforced by a DB `CHECK` |\n| \"Unpublish\" vs CONTEXT.md's *Avoid: Unpublished* | **Kept**: it is the verb the spec uses. The resulting status is labelled **Draft** |\n\n### Spec: all acceptance criteria met\n\n| Finding | Resolution |\n|---|---|\n| Scope creep: the public `/` linked to `data-bites/<slug>.html`, which 404s until T07 | **Fixed**: `/` lists published Data Bite titles without links, and the home template links only when an item has an `href`. T07 adds the pages and links |\n| Every `sqlite3.IntegrityError` was reported as a duplicate slug | **Fixed**: only a `data_bites.slug` UNIQUE violation becomes the validation error; anything else re-raises |\n| Publish and unpublish bump `updated_at` | **Kept, deliberate**: a status change counts as a change. Author and `created_at` are never written after insert |\n| Draft-leak test covers the live `/` only | **Accepted**: the `site/` draft-leak test belongs to T08 |\n| Spec #1's *Out of Scope* still lists \"per-intern individual accounts (deliberately rejected)\", which contradicts ADR-003 | **Not changed here**: the spec issue needs a one-line edit, flagged to the owner |\n\nTests: `tests/test_t03_data_bites.py` has 22 tests, and T00–T03 total 71 passed. Two tests passed on their first run, so I mutation-checked them to confirm they can fail: the duplicate-slug test (UNIQUE constraint removed) and the CSRF test (`require_csrf` dropped from publish). Both failed as expected.\nEOF\n)\"","description":"Post code-review findings comment on issue #4"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":996,"cache_read_input_tokens":109045,"output_tokens":1245,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":0},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":996,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":1245,"cache_read_input_tokens":109045,"cache_creation_input_tokens":996,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":996},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"wireToolInputs":{"toolu_01DvQfdxcmucDF1PS3aTRbnb":{"command":"cd /Users/davelleat/code/iphs400-mp2-cms && gh issue comment 4 --body \"$(cat <<'EOF'\n## /code-review findings for T03 and how each was resolved\n\nTwo-axis review (Standards and Spec) of the T03 diff against `b9fcf90`.\n\n### Standards: no hard violations\nCSRF is on every new POST. uid":"d0970295-ec98-43c6-b5ba-4b77b6106011","timestamp":"2026-09-28T22:08:35.056Z","advisorModel":"claude-opus-5-5","effort":"high","perTurnEffort":"high","session_id":"fc138a42-1eee-4017-bff2-c6a77302f820","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/davelleat/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"fc138a42-1eee-4017-bff2-c6a77302f820","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"d0970295-ec98-43c6-b5ba-4b77b6106011","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-5-5","id":"msg_011CfWbkwoBxSMW6azXfxYvF","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"T03 (Data Bites) is built and committed locally on `main` as `4b4d114`. The commit hasn't been pushed, so issue #4 is still open; it will close when you push, because the commit message ends `Closes #4`.\n\n**What it does**\n- **Where:** a new admin page at `/admin/data-bites`, linked from the admin home for everyone who is signed in.\n- **Create and edit:** any Analyst or Director can create a Data Bite (it always starts as a draft) and edit any Data Bite, including one another Analyst wrote. Editing keeps the original author and creation date; only the \"last updated\" time changes.\n- **Publish and unpublish:** anyone signed in can do both.\n- **Delete:** Director only. The server refuses an Analyst with a 403, not just by hiding the button, and an Analyst never sees the button.\n- **Slugs:** a duplicate slug, on create or on edit, is rejected with a clear error message.\n- **Drafts stay private:** the public home page only ever asks for published Data Bites. It lists their titles without links for now, because the public pages behind those links arrive with T07.\n- **CSRF:** every form that changes data requires the token.\n\n**Tests**\n- `tests/test_t03_data_bites.py` has 22 tests, written test-first through the app's real HTTP routes. T00–T03 all pass (71 tests).\n- The duplicate-slug and CSRF tests passed the first time they ran, so I temporarily broke the code to confirm each one does fail when it should.\n- I also started the server on a throwaway database, so your `cms.db` wasn't touched. The public home returned 200 and `/admin/data-bites` sent an anonymous visitor to `/login`.\n- 14 tests fail in `test_ctx_guard.py` and `test_usage_report.py`. They're course-scaffold stubs that aren't written yet (they raise `NotImplementedError`), and this change doesn't touch them.\n\n**Code review:** the findings and how each was handled are posted on [issue #4](https://github.com/DavelleAT/iphs400-mp2-cms/issues/4#issuecomment-5879631285). There were no hard standards violations and every acceptance criterion was met. I fixed these:\n- The public home was linking to Data Bite pages that don't exist until T07, so those links would have 404'd.\n- Every database constraint error was being reported as a duplicate slug.\n- There was a redundant not-found check, a leftover validation, a wrong docstring and two unclear function names.\n\n**Decision for you:** publishing and unpublishing also update a Data Bite's \"last updated\" time. I treated a status change as a change, and the spec doesn't say either way. Tell me if you'd rather only edits update it.\n\n**Needs a fix in the spec:** the \"Out of Scope\" section of the spec issue (#1) still says per-intern accounts were \"deliberately rejected\", which contradicts ADR-003 and the rest of the spec. I left that one-line edit to you.\n\nI left `notes/usage-ledger.csv` and the two new transcripts out of the commit, as the T02 commit did."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"end_turn","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":886,"cache_read_input_tokens":111355,"output_tokens":1065,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":43},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":886,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":1065,"cache_read_input_tokens":111355,"cache_creation_input_tokens":886,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":886},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"apiBlockIndex":1,"requestId":"req_011CfWbkwL9i2ZVCivDqS1g3","type":"assistant","uuid":"4d127403-67dd-4336-b7d4-96bcd9637f59","timestamp":"2026-09-28T22:08:38.094Z","advisorModel":"claude-opus-5-5","effort":"high","perTurnEffort":"high","session_id":"fc138a42-1eee-4017-bff2-c6a77302f820","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/davelleat/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"fc138a42-1eee-4017-bff2-c6a77302f820","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}
